```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

```java
public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

```

    //@ assigns \nothing;

    public class IsUndirectedGraph {
        //@ requires adjacencyMat != null;
        //@ requires adjacencyMat.length > 0;
        //@ requires \forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1;
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
        //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
        public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
            int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
                //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
                for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                    if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }

    //@ also
    //@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;

    public class IsUndirectedGraph {
        //@ requires adjacencyMat != null;
        //@ requires adjacencyMat.length > 0;
        //@ requires \forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1;
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
        //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
        public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
            int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
                //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
                for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                    if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }

    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ also
    //@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;

    public class IsUndirectedGraph {
        //@ requires adjacencyMat != null;
        //@ requires adjacencyMat.length > 0;
        //@ requires \forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1;
        //@ assigns \nothing;
        //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
        //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
        public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
            int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
                //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
                for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                    if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
    }

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
//@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
//@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
//@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ assigns \nothing;

//@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//@ also
//@ requires adjacencyMat == null;
//@ assigns \nothing;
//@ ensures \result == false;
//@ behaviors disjoint;
//@ behaviors complete;

public class IsUndirectedGraph {
    //@ requires adjacencyMat != null && adjacencyMat.length > 0;
    //@ requires (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < adjacencyMat.length && 0 <= j && j < adjacencyMat[i].length; 0 <= adjacencyMat[i][j] && adjacencyMat[i][j] <= 1);
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result ==> (\forall int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] == adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    //@ ensures !\result ==> (\exists int i, j; 0 <= i && i < nVert && 0 <= j && j < nVert; adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]);
    public static boolean isUndirectedGraph(int[][] adjacencyMat) {
        int nVert = adjacencyMat.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
        for (int i = 0; i < nVert; i++) {
            //@ maintaining \forall int k, l; 0 <= k && k < i && 0 <= l && l < nVert; adjacencyMat[k][l] == adjacencyMat[l][k];
            for (int j = i + 1; j < nVert; j++) {
                if (adjacencyMat[i][j] != adjacencyMat[j][i]) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}
